Creating EFW2 Submitter Codes

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/EFW2-Submitter-Maint.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/EFW2-Submitter-Maint.htm

Creating EFW2 Submitter Codes

For EFW2 files, the first record is the RA record that requires submitter information. Since this data is not specific to an employer or individual user, you must manually enter the submitter information in the EFW2 Submitter Codes form.

The Social Security Administration (SSA) requires that all Employers be reported in one file, with exceptions. If you believe you have an exception and want to limit the export file to specific Employers, add them to the list in the Employer List Maintenance section.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

To create EFW2 submitter codes:

  1. On the Work Centers panel, click Tax > W-2 Processing > W-2 Setup > Parameter Setup > EFW2 Submitter Codes. The EFW2 Submitter Codes page opens.

  2. Enter or select the Submitter ID. If only one submitter is used accept DEFAULT. Otherwise select the specific submitter.

  3. Make any necessary changes to the information that the system supplies based on the Submitter ID.

Option — Description

Submitter Name — The name of the company that is reporting the annual W-2 wages.

Submitter EIN — The submitter’s Employer Identification Number.

Location Address — The submitter’s location address (Attention, Suite, Room Number, and so forth).

Delivery Address — The submitter’s delivery address (Street or Post Office Box).

City — The submitter’s city address.

State — The submitter’s 2-digit state address.

Zip Code — The submitter’s zip code, inclulding the 4-digit zip code extension if applicable.

Contact Name — The name of the person that the SSA should contact concerning processing problems.

Telephone — The contact’s telephone number, including the area code. Include the extension number if applicable.

Email — The contact’s email address.

Fax — The contact’s fax number, including the area code.

Preferred Contact Method — Email or Postal Service.

Personal Submitter PIN — The personal identification number assigned to the employee who is authorized to submit this file.

Employer List Maintenance — If you want to limit the export file to specific Employers, add them to the list by clicking the Employee ID link.

  1. Click Save.

This form displays the totals of tax amounts and tax basis for Federal, State, and Local taxes.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

Field — Description

Zip Code Above or Equal To — Five-digit zip code for an employee that is above or equal to a specific value.

Zip Code Below or Equal To — Five-digit zip code for an employee that is below or equal to a specific value.

Entering External W-2 Benefits

Use the External W-2 Benefit Entry form to enter benefit premium amounts that are not tracked or calculated in PrismHR. When a user enters data and saves the form, the values are written to the employee’s W-2 register so that they can be shown on the employee’s W-2 form under box 12 code DD.

Employer ID — Enter one of more employers and the Externally Reported Premium for each employer. The system supplies the employer name.

Importing External W-2 Benefits

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Employee-W2-Benefit-Import.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Employee-W2-Benefit-Import.htm

Importing External W-2 Benefits

Use the External W-2 Benefits Import form to import data for external benefits that need to be displayed on the employee’s Form W-2. The imported benefit amount data does not change the benefits register created as a result of posting payrolls. The data affects only the Employee W-2 Benefits Report.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group security.

Import File

The file format for the imported data file is as follows:

Column 1 Company ID
Column 2 Employee ID
Column 3 W-2 Class
Column 4 External Benefit Amount
Column 5 Year
Column 6 Month
Column 7 Employer ID

Procedure

To import external W-2 benefits:

  1. Open the Work Centers menu and select Work Centers|Tax > W-2 Processing.

  2. Under W-2 Processes, click External W-2 Benefit Import. The External W-2 Benefit Import page opens.

  3. Fill in the form.

Option — Description

Upload File — Click the link, click Choose File, select the data file, and then click Open > submit. When you are notified that the file was uploaded, click Close. The system displays a preview of the data.

Delimiter — Select Auto, Comma, Tab, or Pipe. If you select Auto, the system selects the delimiter based on the file content.

Qualifier — Select Auto, None, ", or '.

First Lines to Skip — Select the number of lines to skip at the beginning of the file. For example, the first three lines may provide instructions or overview information.

Reimport — Select this option to reimport a data file that was previously imported.

  1. Click Import. A progress box displays the status.

  2. When the progress box shows that the process is complete, click Close to return to the External W-2 Benefit Import page or Report to open the Job Control Status Report.

  • All employees including US territory residents (the default)
  • All employees excluding US territory residents
  • US territory residents only | Output Options | > Note: Note: These options only display if the Global Configuration option, “Build W2s From Client Federal Tax Registers”, is enabled. Handling Errors

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Error%20Handling.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Error Handling.htm

Handling Errors

Since W-2 processing runs as a background task, the system provides various ways to inform you when errors occur, even if you are working on another task during W-2 processing. The type of notification method used depends on the severity of the errors that are encountered.

Pre-Processing Errors

When you select Create Employee W-2 forms, the system runs a quick check and displays a list of errors that you must address before processing can occur. When processing is complete, you receive an email notification.

Non Fatal errors

These are errors on employee related data or setup parameters. The system saves errors in the error log.

Fatal errors

Fatal errors stop the W-2 build process. When a fatal occurs during processing, you receive an email notification. To see the error, open the error log from the Create W2 form.

Error Report

The system records errors that are detected during W-2 processing in an error report that it generates when the build process is complete. You can see the errors by opening the W-2 Processing Status form. The error report shows the following information:

  • Number of errors found so far

  • Number of W-2s generated so far

Processing Flow for Errors

The following charts show the process flow for each type of error.

Pre-Processing Errors Fatal Errors Non-Fatal Errors
[Image: screenshot] [Image: screenshot] [Image: screenshot]

Maintaining Local EFW2 Codes

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Local-EFW2-Codes.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Local-EFW2-Codes.htm

Maintaining Local EFW2 Codes

EFW2 is a type of format that employers and payroll providers use to report W2 forms electronically with the SSA (Social Security Administration) and state and local governments. Each year, the SSA updates the W-2 electronic filing instructions through the EFW2 specification.

Use the Local EFW2 form to generate and store local EFW2 codes. These codes are assigned to the tax authority for local government and used to build the W-2 registers. The codes are then used in the EFW2 build process to denote which taxes for the applicable tax authority should be included on the EFW2 that is sent to the local government. You can view, add, and delete codes.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

To maintain local EFW2 codes:

  1. On the Work Centers panel, click Tax > W-2 Processing > W-2 Setup > Parameter Setup > Local EFW2 Codes. The Local EFW2 Codes page opens. One column shows the list of codes and the other column shows the description of each code.

  2. Select one of the following options:

  • To insert a blank row above the selected row, click > at the beginning of the row and then enter the code and description in the new row.

  • To insert a blank row at the end of the list, click + at the top of the list and then enter the code and description in the new row.

  • To delete a code, click X at the beginning of the row. The system deletes the row.

  1. Click Save.

========================================================================

W-2 Processing Overview

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Overview%20of%20W-2%20Processing.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Overview of W-2 Processing.htm

W-2 Processing Overview

This section gives a summary overview of W-2 processing. It is best practice to read it completely before you begin.

W-2 Setup

There are several setup forms for processing W2s.

Use this form — To do this

EFW2 Submitter Maintenance — Specify where PEOs report their W-2 data on the electronic files they send to various government agencies. The process of generating the W-2 data starts with the Create Employee W-2 Forms option, which creates the W-2 data and registers. You can specify whether to create W-2 data for all employers or for selected employers.

EFW2 Local List Maintenance — Create and maintain local EFW2 codes. You then assign these codes to the relevant local tax authorities. When the W-2 data is created, the system places the code on the employee’s W-2 register and then reads it when building the EFW2 files for upload to the various local governments.

Benefits W-2 Reporting Setup — Specify the types of benefit plans that need to be reported on the W-2 in Box 12.

Global W-2 Reporting Setup — Set up the reporting codes for external benefit plans. For example, assume that the employee health insurance that needs to be in Box 12 is not a PEO based plan and is calculated outside of the PrismHR system. In this scenario the benefit plan type codes are set up so that the PEO can enter the premium amounts either manually or via an import.

Client W2 Reporting Setup — Creates an exclusion for clients to report their benefits in Box 12. Consult PrismHR Support before using this functionality.

W2 Processing

The W2 Processing menu is where you build and verify the W-2 registers.

Use this form — To do this

Create Employee W-2 Forms — Build the registers that subsequent steps in the W-2 process will use.

Update W2 Record — Manually add or edit data to the W-2 register. This form is also commonly used to verify the data contained in the employee’s W2 register. The system populates this form with the W2 register data that was created using the Create W2 form.

Employee W2 Benefits Entry — Manually enter external benefit premiums on an employee by employee basis. enter the benefit premium amounts monthly. - You can create an individual record for each month using a multi-value grid where you enter each month’s premium at one time. - You can also enter a year’s worth of premiums under one month to get total premiums for a year.

Employee W2 Benefit Import — Import data for external benefit premiums instead of entering it manually. This is typically used for a client who has external benefits and gets the information from a benefits administrator.

Employee W2 Benefits Inquiry — Look at the employee’s W2 benefit data. Typically you would run the employee W2 Benefit Report, which provides the same data for a client instead of an employee.

Electronic Files

The system provides a variety of electronic file formats that you can generate for federal, state, and local governments. You can also generate electronic files for third party printers such as CIC and Solv. The files that the system generates are imported into one of the third party providers, who then print, stuff, and send them to PEOs to distribute to their employees.

Use this form — To do this

EFW2 Download — Build the EFW2 data. When you open this form, you are prompted to specify whether it is for the federal, state, or local government.

Create BDS/IBF W2 Download — Send data to SOLV so that they can print, stuff, and mail the employee W-2 forms.

Create CIC W2 Download — Send the W2 information to CIC so that they can print, stuff, and mail the employee W-2 forms.

CIC PR Download — Provide additional information required for Puerto Rico employees.

CIC Virgin Islands Download — Provide additional information required for Virgin IIsland employees.

========================================================================

Updating Employee W-2c Records

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Update-EE-W-2C.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Update-EE-W-2C.htm

Updating Employee W-2c Records

You can update employee W-2c register data with this form. If an employee does not have a W-2c, the system displays a message that there is no W-2c record for the values entered and instead loads a W-2 record. The employee wages are shown on the update W-2 and update W-2c forms without having to enter a client federal entity ID.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

To update this form:

  1. Open the Work Centers menu and select Work Centers|Tax > W-2 Processing.

  2. Under W-2 Processes, select Update Employee W-2C Forms.

  3. Complete these fields as required:

Field Description
Employer ID of the employer.
SBEA Entity Optional field that only displays when Build W2s from Client Federal Tax Registers is enabled (See Setting Global Configuration). This is a client federal entity, who represents a client of your organization that is included in SBEA reporting.
Employee ID of the employee this form pertains to.
Tax Year Tax year for this W-2c.
Record This field shows you if you have already generated EFW2C record for the employee or not. The following options are available depending on the status of the EFW2C record for the employee: - 1 (Create New) - you need to create a EFW2C record for this employee since none exists in the system - all fields In the next section will be blank. - 1 (Active) - a EFW2C record for this employee exists. If the status of the file containing this employee EFW2C record is: - Unsent - a EFW2C record for this employee exists but it has not been submitted to the SSA - all fields in the next section are pre-populated but cannot be edited. - In Process - a EFW2C record for this employee exists and it has been submitted to the SSA, but has not been accepted yet - all fields in the next section are pre-populated but cannot be edited. - Accepted - a EFW2C record for this employee exists and it has been submitted to the SSA, and has been accepted - all fields in the next section are pre-populated but cannot be edited. - Rejected - a EFW2C record for this employee exists and it was submitted to the SSA, but was rejected - all fields in the next section are pre-populated and can be edited. - Regenerate - a EFW2C record for this employee was submitted to the SSA but need to be updated and re-submitted for some reason - all fields in the next section are pre-populated and can be edited. - Cancelled - a EFW2C record for this employee was cancelled - all fields In the next section will be blank.
  • 1 (Create New) - you need to create a EFW2C record for this employee since none exists in the system - all fields In the next section will be blank.
  • 1 (Active) - a EFW2C record for this employee exists. If the status of the file containing this employee EFW2C record is:
  • Unsent - a EFW2C record for this employee exists but it has not been submitted to the SSA - all fields in the next section are pre-populated but cannot be edited.
  • In Process - a EFW2C record for this employee exists and it has been submitted to the SSA, but has not been accepted yet - all fields in the next section are pre-populated but cannot be edited.
  • Accepted - a EFW2C record for this employee exists and it has been submitted to the SSA, and has been accepted - all fields in the next section are pre-populated but cannot be edited.
  • Rejected - a EFW2C record for this employee exists and it was submitted to the SSA, but was rejected - all fields in the next section are pre-populated and can be edited.
  • Regenerate - a EFW2C record for this employee was submitted to the SSA but need to be updated and re-submitted for some reason - all fields in the next section are pre-populated and can be edited.
  • Cancelled - a EFW2C record for this employee was cancelled - all fields In the next section will be blank. | Social Security Number | Employee SSN in NNN-NN-NNNN format. | | First Name/Middle Initial/Last Name/Suffix | An employee’s first name, middle initial, last name, and whatever suffix they use. For example, Jr for Junior. | | Street Address/City, State, Zip Code | An employee’s street address, city, state, and zip code. | | Wages, tips, other compensation | Total wages paid to an employee that are subject to federal income tax. | | Social Security Wages | Total wages paid to an employee that are subject to social security tax. | | Medicare Wages and Tips | Total wages paid to an employee that are subject to Medicare tax. | | Social Security Tips | Amount of tips reported to an employer that is subject to this tax. | | Nonqualified Plans | State and local agencies should separately identify section 457 distributions in this box by using code “G”. | | Statutory Employee | Enable this setting to print an X in this check box on the W-2c form. Statutory employees report earnings on Schedule C, where allowable expenses can be reported along with regular income. | | Retirement Plan | Enable this setting to print an X in this check box on the W-2c form. This box is checked if an employee was an “active participant” for any part of the year in a qualified pension, profit-sharing, or stock-bonus plan. | | Third-Party Sick Pay | Enable this setting to print an X in this check box on the W-2c form. Third party sick pay is an insurance disability benefit (payment) that provides benefits to employees in place of lost wages due to absences caused by an illness or a non-work related injury. | | Federal Income Tax Withheld | Total amount of federal income tax withheld. | | Social Security Withheld | Total amount of social security tax withheld. | | Medicare Tax Withheld | Total amount of Medicare tax withheld. | | Allocated Tips | Amounts an employer assigned to an employee in addition to the tips reported. | | Dependent Care Benefits | Reports any dependent care amounts. | | Box 12 | These codes provide more information and determine if the amount is income, such as health insurance premiums. However, if any amount is gross income, it’s already included in W-2c Box 1. Each code is either a single or double letter code. Enter a code and an amount. Detailed information on Box 12 codes can be found here. | | Box 14 Other | Use this box to report information such as state disability insurance taxes withheld, union dues, health insurance premiums deducted, and so forth. Enter a description and an amount. | | Box 15-17 | Use this to report taxable earnings for a state and the amount of income tax withheld. Includes the state’s EIN code and the UI number. | | Box 18-20 | Use this to report local wages and income tax for localities that collect local taxes. | | Benefits Inquiry | | | Benefit Companies | Companies set up to report benefits on Form W-2c at the time this form was generated for the current employee. | | Benefit Plans | Lists benefit plans and W-2c classes to be included on this form. | | FSA Accounts | FSA account types with match amounts being reported on Form W-2c. | | Total | The total amount of benefits plus the FSA match. |
  1. Click Save.

Updating Employee W-2 Records

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Update_Employee-W-2-Record.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Update_Employee-W-2-Record.htm

Updating Employee W-2 Records

Update an employee’s W-2 data with Update Employee W-2 Forms. The employee wages are shown on the update W-2 and update W-2c forms without having to enter a client federal entity ID.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

  1. Open the Work Centers menu and select Work Centers|Tax > W-2 Processing.

  2. Under W-2 Processes, select Update Employee W-2 Forms.

  3. Update these fields as required.

Field — Description

Employer — ID of the employer.

SBEA Entity — Optional field that only displays when Build W2s from Client Federal Tax Registers is enabled (See Setting Global Configuration). This is a client federal entity, who represents a client of your organization that is included in SBEA reporting.

Employee — ID of the employee this form pertains to.

Tax Year — Tax year for this W-2.

W-2 Manually Updated — This box is automatically checked when data is updated on the W-2 record. It is also used during the W-2 creation process to skip a W-2.

Social Security Number — Employee SSN in NNN-NN-NNNN format.

First Name/Middle Initial/Last Name/Suffix — An employee’s first name, middle initial, last name, and whatever suffix they use. For example, Jr for Junior.

Street Address/City, State, Zip Code — An employee’s street address, city, state, and zip code.

Wages, tips, other compensation — Total wages paid to an employee that are subject to federal income tax.

Social Security Wages — Total wages paid to an employee that are subject to social security tax.

Medicare Wages and Tips — Total wages paid to an employee that are subject to Medicare tax.

Social Security Tips — Amount of tips reported to an employer that is subject to this tax.

Nonqualified Plans — State and local agencies should separately identify section 457 distributions in this box by using code “G”.

Statutory Employee — Check to print an X in this check box on the W-2 form. Statutory employees report earnings on Schedule C, where allowable expenses can be reported along with regular income.

Retirement Plan — Check to print an X in this check box on the W-2 form. Check the retirement plan box if an employee was an “active participant” for any part of the year in a qualified pension, profit-sharing, or stock-bonus plan.

Third-Party Sick Pay — Check to print an X in this check box on the W-2 form. Third party sick pay is an insurance disability benefit (payment) that provides benefits to employees in place of lost wages due to absences caused by an illness or a non-work related injury.

Federal Income Tax Withheld — Total amount of federal income tax withheld.

Social Security Withheld — Total amount of social security tax withheld.

Medicare Tax Withheld — Total amount of Medicare tax withheld.

Allocated Tips — Amounts an employer assigned to an employee in addition to the tips reported.

Dependent Care Benefits — Reports any dependent care amounts.

Box 12 — These codes provide more information and determine if the amount is income, such as health insurance premiums. However, if any amount is gross income, it’s already included in W-2 Box 1. Each code is either a single or double letter code. Enter a code and an amount. Detailed information on Box 12 codes can be found here.

Box 14 Other — Use this box to report information such as state disability insurance taxes withheld, union dues, health insurance premiums deducted, and so forth. Enter a description and an amount. From tax year 2024, for both SBEA and Non-SBEA clients, the YTD employee portion of the Vermont Child Care Contribution (tax code 46-27) will be added to Box 14 on the Form W-2 and Form W-2c generated by the system. This will report as VT CCC with the dollar amount.

Box 15-17 — Use this to report taxable earnings for a state and the amount of income tax withheld. Includes the state’s EIN code and the UI number.

Box 18-20 — Use this to report local wages and income tax for localities that collect local taxes.

Benefits Inquiry

Benefit Companies — Companies set up to report benefits on W-2s at the time this form was generated for the current employee.

Benefit Plans — Lists benefit plans and W-2 classes to be included on this form.

FSA Accounts — FSA account types with match amounts being reported on W-2s.

Client Inquiry - This section will only populate for tax year 2025 and onwards. When the Form W-2 data is built on the Employer and the associated client information is added to the build, the Client ID, Company Name, and Federal Taxable Wages will appear as read only on this new form. This information cannot be edited by the user. If an employee is associated with multiple clients, each client appears as a separate row with accurate federal taxable wage allocation. Only users with permission to view/update employee Form W-2s can access the Client Inquiry section.

Client ID — The client ID/s the employee is associated with.

Company Name — The company names/s the employee is associated with.

Federal Taxable Wages — The federal taxable wages of the employee for the client for the tax year.

  1. Click Save.

========================================================================

Job Control Status

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/Viewing%20the%20Job%20Control%20Status%20Report.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/Viewing the Job Control Status Report.htm

Viewing the Job Control Status Report

When a process is complete, you may have the option to access a Job Control Status report. This report shows the Job Number, the date and time when it started and stopped, and the job status. It also shows the date and time that each step in the process was executed.

To open the Job Control Status report, click Report in the message that informs you that processing is complete.

You have the following options:

  • Navigate through multiple pages using arrows to move forward, back, to the beginning, or to the end.

  • Go directly to a page by typing the page number in the Page box.

  • Enter search text in the Search box. Click Search Next to find the next occurrence.

  • In the Actions menu, select Print, Convert to PDF, or Print/PDF Pages.

  • Click XLS to export the report to Excel format and save it in your Downloads folder.

========================================================================

Form W-2 FSA Account Type Reporting

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/W-2_FSA_Account-Type-Reporting.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/W-2_FSA_Account-Type-Reporting.htm

Form W-2 FSA Account Type Reporting

Determine the FSA plans you want to report on Form W-2 Box 12.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

To determine the plans to report:

  1. On the Work Centers panel, click Tax > W-2 Processing > W-2 Setup > Parameter Setup > W-2 FSA Account Type Reporting.

  2. Review the Plan IDs.

  3. Select Yes for each Plan ID you want to report on the W-2.

  4. Click Save.

========================================================================

Setting Up Benefit Plans for the W-2 Form

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/W2-Benefit-Plan-Setup.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/W2-Benefit-Plan-Setup.htm

Setting Up Benefit Plans for the W-2 Form

Use the W2 Benefit Plan Setup form to specify the types of group benefit plans to display under box 12 on the employee’s W-2 form.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

To set up the benefit plans to display on the W-2 form:

  1. Open the Work Centers menu. Under Work Centers|Tax, click W-2 Processing.

  2. Under W-2 Setup, click W2 Benefit Plan Setup. The W2 Benefit Plan Setup page opens.

  3. Complete the form.

Insurance Class — Select Yes or No in the Include column to specify whether to include each class. (The system uses a fixed list of insurance classes, which cannot be edited.)

Report on W2 Equals — Select Yes to show plans currently set up to report on W-2s. Select No to show plans currently set up to not report on W-2s. Select Both to show all plans to report on W-2s.

Payee ID Equals — If you only want to display plans for a specific payee, enter the payee ID here. Otherwise, leave this field blank.

Sort Benefit Plans By — Select Plan ID or Description to specify how to sort benefit plans.

  1. Select Build List. The system displays the Plan ID, Description, and Report On W2 columns for the specified plans. (You cannot change the items that display in the Plan ID column.)

Note: Note the following:

  • After you set the parameters in the W2 Benefit Plan Setup panel and select Build List, a “Please wait” message briefly displays while the background process builds the benefit plans list. If the number of plans that display exceeds 100, the form loads the plans list in pages of 100 plans. (You can use the Previous Page and Next Page buttons or Go To Page to quickly navigate through the plans that display.)

  • If you update the Include column after the list builds and then select Build List a message displays, “You will lose any edits if you rebuild the list. Are you sure?” Click Yes to continue.

  1. Select either Yes or No in the Report On W2 column for each plan ID individually or click Set All to Yes or Set All to No.

Note: When a list has multiple pages, the Set All To Yes, Set All To No, and Save functions apply to all pages in the list, not just to the page that currently displays.

  1. Click Save. (You can click Cancel to clear the entered parameters and the plan list without having to exit the form.)

========================================================================

W-2 Processing

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/W2-Landing-Page.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/W2-Landing-Page.htm

W-2 Processing

The W-2 processing options provide everything needed in the life cycle of W-2 processing, from setup to printing. In addition, these menus provide the flexibility to perform small and specific adjustment processes, as well as to identify larger tax issues and use automated adjustment processes to correct them.

The W-2 processes provide a straightforward user experience to handle the myriad of complicated tax-related processes and calculations needed to assure that you calculate and report your employees’ and clients’ taxes. A specific focus is on turning large, time-consuming projects into more automated procedures that require less time to complete.

The options available in the W-2 Processing work center are organized under the following headings:

  • W-2 Processes

  • EFW2 Exports

  • 3rd Party W-2 Export Files

  • W-2 Reporting

  • W-2 Setup Reports

  • W-2 Setup

  • Parameter Setup

  • W-2 Printing

========================================================================

Viewing the W-2 Record Change Log

Source: https://tech-docs.prismhr.com/SP_help/Content/CMP/Year-End/W-2%20Processing/W2-Record-Change-Log.htm Path: CMP/Year-End/W-2 Processing/W2-Record-Change-Log.htm

Viewing the W-2 Record Change Log

Track manual changes to a W-2 register with the W-2 Record Change Log form. You must have access to the Update Employee W-2 form to see the change log.

Note: Note: This form does not use Client Access Group Security.

  1. Select the Work Centers menu and under Work Centers|Tax, select W-2 Processing.

  2. Under W-2 Processes, select Update Employee W-2 Forms. The form displays.

  3. Open the Actions menu [Image: screenshot] and select Change Log.

[Image: screenshot]

  1. Review the log. Click OK to close the form.
